				has_country_flag = AUH_old_man_dead
			}
		}
		completion_reward = {
			add_political_power = 75
			add_stability = 0.05
			set_country_flag = AUH_x_regency_prepared
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = AUH_old_man_dead }
				add_political_power = 50
				add_stability = 0.05
				custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_x_regency_now_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_x_regency_prepared_tt
			}
		}
	}

	focus = {
		id = AUHDNC_x_the_regiments_in_the_streets
		relative_position_id = AUHDNC_x_a_cabinet_without_a_chamber
		x = -2
		y = 2
		prerequisite = {
			focus = AUHDNC_x_a_cabinet_without_a_chamber
			focus = AUHDNC_x_the_sixty_eight_words
		}
		icon = GFX_focus_generic_oppression
		cost = 3
		allow_branch = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = AUH_clock_bell_kingdom
				has_country_flag = AUH_clock_bell_street
			}
		}
		select_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { var = AUH_county_resistance value = 5 }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = AUH_county_resistance
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_country_flag = AUH_dnc_path_sword
			}
		}
		completion_reward = {
			add_war_support = 0.05
			add_stability = -0.05
			subtract_from_variable = { var = AUH_gemeinsamkeit value = 3 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = AUH_gemeinsamkeit
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_a_gemeinsamkeit_down_tt
			subtract_from_variable = { var = AUH_strike_pressure value = 15 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = AUH_strike_pressure
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_x_counties_stir_tt
			set_country_flag = AUH_x_regiments_deployed
			custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_x_regiments_tt
		}
	}

	focus = {
		id = AUHDNC_x_the_peace_of_the_king
		x = 162
		y = 11
		prerequisite = { focus = AUHDNC_the_dual_monarchy }
		icon = GFX_focus_generic_monarchy_1
		cost = 5
		allow_branch = { has_country_flag = AUH_x_the_kingdom_beaten }
		ai_will_do = { factor = 10 }
		completion_reward = {
			add_political_power = 150
			add_stability = 0.10
			add_war_support = 0.05
			add_to_variable = { var = HUN_support value = -4 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = HUN_support
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = AUH_gemeinsamkeit value = 4 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = AUH_gemeinsamkeit
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_a_gemeinsamkeit_down_tt
			set_country_flag = AUH_x_peace_of_the_king
			set_country_flag = AUH_dnc_era_end
			custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_x_era_end_peace_tt
		}
	}
